Snapshot","stage":"before_G","root":"RawValidationSubject","slots":["effect_resource","recovery_usage","flow_execution"],"source_node_target":"NativeRelationSnapshotSource","none_source_nodes":0,"c_source_domain":"g_external"},"canonical_constraints":["Untrusted assertion descriptors use the deterministic **Raw(T)** companion family: transform each text/string/identifier/digest-spelling scalar position into exact UTF8 bytes, preserving its original nominal field/model tag; keep actual byte fields as bytes, fixed-width native numerics as their exact nominal widths, option tags and actual native enum cases; recursively transform every field/case payload; preserve every Vec as sequence, including bad assertion order. Native set values retain their defined set semantics. Freeze the complete resulting companion descriptors in RM, not a runtime reflection transform. Thus Raw(CompilerClaim),Raw(Context),Raw(binding) retain invalid/NFD compiler labels,paths,pins and names without normalizing them. Checked owner/source subjects use ordinary validated descriptors. Comparing a claimed text scalar to the owner compares exact UTF8 bytes, not raw/checked wrapper tags or normalized text. A truly unknown native model case still fails UnsupportedModel; unknown transport cases fail earlier. This family gives byte-exact claim preservation without an opaque serialized object or a natural-language parser.","Kind ranks1–25, exactly: assertion_input,exact_pin,proof_context,resolver_provenance,compiler_identity,subflow_dependency,input_mapping,output_producer,failure_containment,effect_containment,resource_mapping,information_flow,usage_coverage,recovery_coverage,execution_source,execution_feasibility,execution_security,body_conformance,implementation_conformance,scope_authority,scope_budget,runtime_authority,publisher_authentication,control_evidence,prerequisite. Successor appends substitution_evidence at rank26;old ranks remain unchanged.","Dimension = none(rank0), I1,O2,Phi3,epsilon4,R5,F6,U7,K8,X9, or supporting{predicate:PredicateId}. Supporting cases compare after root dimensions by the closed predicate case text. Role ranks follow section2; they are derived and need not appear in Identity again. Successor appends substitution:SubstitutionDimension after supporting;its twelve cases use the registered numeric order.","Requirement={clause:RequirementClause,evidence_class:EvidenceClass}.\nRequirementClause is a closed tagged sum direct:DirectKind | recovery:RecoveryClause | control:ControlClause | prerequisite:PrerequisitePredicate. DirectKind is precisely kinds1–13 and15–23, in kind-rank order. The other three payload enums are the following ordered lists. The enclosing kind must agree with the clause family and direct payload. This explicit tag avoids conflating identically named control/direct clauses in a context-free codec.\nFor kinds1–13 and15–23, clause is that kind's tag. Recovery14 clauses in order: source_closure,action_binding,usage_profile,multiplicity,guard_compatibility,epsilon_coverage,recovery_usage_coverage,eligibility,commit_order.\nControl24: resolved_leaf,topology,output_interface,selector_totality,effect_envelope,failure_row,resource_provenance,information_flow,usage,retry_safety,child_settlement,forward_history,compensation_coverage,root_export_boundary,unsupported_control,recovery_blocked_outcome.\nPrerequisite25: closed_contract_schema,imported_module_closure,schema_universe,positive_unique_ports,failure_pins,effect_operation_membership,resource_universe,effect_resource_relation,flow_catalogs,io_resource_namespaces,additive_meter_units,recovery_usage_exhaustiveness,closed_effect_action_coverage,exact_k_identity,execution_universes,flow_execution_relation,required_dimensions,exact_relation_pins. Successor appends substitution:SubstitutionClause with exactly the26 registered non-context cases;context_association remains direct:proof_context.","EvidenceClass ranks: static_declaration,typed_counterexample_resolution,trusted_body,implementation_attestation,provider_receipt,authority_attestation,publisher_authentication,control_terminal_state,committed_effect_order,recovery_outcome,provider_honoring,application_idempotency. Ordinary violations KEEP the predicate's evidence class; they do not automatically become typed_counterexample_resolution.","Allowed class relation is predicate-specific, not merely clause-wide: static predicates use static_declaration; body/implementation use trusted_body/implementation_attestation; runtime/publisher use authority_attestation/publisher_authentication; Scope authority/budget static_declaration with Unproven; actual owned-child settlement uses control_terminal_state, while static timeout-outcome separation and passive-AWAIT declarations use static_declaration; commit_order uses committed_effect_order; recovery_blocked_outcome uses recovery_outcome; compensation eligibility uses provider_receipt; retry facts distinguish static declarations, provider_honoring and application_idempotency predicates by their actual evidence query. Counterexample_resolution is only an explicitly declared prerequisite asking to resolve a typed contradiction. No unsupported cross-product is accepted.","PredicateFamily = root_preflight | root_dimension | execution_child | root_boundary | leaf | control | root_recovery | prerequisite. ClosedPredicateCase:\n- root_preflight: assertion_input,exact_pin,proof_context,compiler_identity,subflow_dependency.\n- root_dimension: I,O,Phi,epsilon,R,F,U,K,X.\n- execution_child: source,feasibility,security.\n- root_boundary: body,implementation,scope_authority,scope_budget,runtime,publisher; subflow-body is leaf/body_conformance under its exact call path.\n- prerequisite: the18 clauses above plus conditional-applicability queries epsilon_nonempty and flow_constraints_require_relation (support predicates, not new arbitrary missing categories).\n- leaf/control/root_recovery: the exact cases in closed_predicates; dynamic wrappers keep the original PredicateId, not the wrapper function name. Each map row binds one of the closed clauses above and its frozen governing RuleRef. No site line number is a runtime case.\nThe RM roster must include both successful and non-successful branch variants and their mandatory source prerequisites; proof aggregation priority is never a substitute for this binding. For this successor the family roster additionally appends standalone_substitution with exactly the27 case_descriptors;the preceding closed roster describes inherited families only.","Compiler={analysis_semantics_version:Text,registry_content:Bytes,rule_registry_content:Bytes,specifications:Seq<SpecificationContentIdentity>,rules:Seq<RuleContentIdentity>,support_manifest:FullSupportManifest}; each SpecificationContentIdentity={specification_id,logical_path,content_sha256,content:Bytes}; each RuleContentIdentity={rule_id,normative_source,source_anchor,short_statement}. These vectors retain original assertion sequence order when untrusted; owner-generated projections use their declared canonical order. All bytes, including complete registry rows not repeated in the four-field rule projection, are retained.","FullSupportManifest={normative_sources:Set<SpecificationContentIdentity>,closed_tables:{hash_domains:Bytes,abi_numbers:Bytes,rule_areas:Bytes,afidl_primitives:Bytes},selected_specifications:Set<SpecificationId>,applied_rules:Set<RuleContentIdentity>,semantic_roles:Seq<SemanticRoleEntry>,attribution:AttributionProfile,report_shape:ModelEdition,obligation_edition:ObligationEdition}. SemanticRoleEntry and AttributionProfile reuse the exact full existing Portable AFIDL types, including all role/dependency/status fields; only the full_content_revalidation case is supported. The owner has all74 source bindings here,48 selected specifications and226 applied rules as specified in the edition plan. This complete support view is recaptured from existing Portable manifest fields/implementation-owned source bytes; it is a new non-wire report model field, NOT a new AFIDL field or a hidden 62->36 content reduction. Owner support is data built from frozen sources, not a report-dependent self-checksum.","CompilerClaim is the closed sum legacy_v1{analysis_semantics_version,registry_content,rule_registry_content,specifications,rules with their original exact types} | successor_v2:Compiler. Assertion input descriptors use Raw(CompilerClaim), rather than demanding a stale v1 claim grow an invented support_manifest or canonical NFC labels. The original full old claim can therefore yield a new-owner compiler-preflight mismatch with its bytes intact. Scope's owner compiler must be successor_v2 and exact supported; these are different tagged comparison operands.","Inherited root/computation Context only (not standalone contract context): Context={compiled_modules:Map<SpecificationId,CompiledModuleContents>,type_schemas:Map<LogicalTypeIdentity,CanonicalTypeSchema>,effect_catalogs:Map<SpecificationId,Set<Text>>,resource_types:{schemas:Map<LogicalTypeIdentity,CanonicalTypeSchema>,descriptors:Map<LogicalTypeIdentity,ResourceTypeDescriptor>},flow_catalogs:{confidentiality:Set<ConfidentialityLabelRef>,taints:Set<TaintRef>,sinks:Map<EffectOperationRef,Bool>},usage_meters:Map<UsageMeterRef,{meter:UsageMeterRef,unit:UsageUnitRef,aggregation:UsageAggregation}>,recovery_operations:{effects:Map<SpecificationId,Set<Text>>,reconciliation_operations:Set<{catalog:Text,name:Text}>},execution_universes:ExecutionUniverseContents,relation_snapshots:Seq<RelationSnapshotContent>,resolver_dependencies:ResolverDependencyManifest}.
